Of the compared pixels 584,199 are not identical in total over the 96 pairs (about 0.6% of them): 559,455 differ by 1 or 2 of 255 (most of them the tour bar's gradient in dark theme, about 32,600 pixels in each of 16 pairs, which is in another compositing layer; the rest the frame's translucent border and toolbar glyphs), 24,744 by more, all of them on glyph edges and frame corners inside the tolerance (H126) |

After the sequence one file under `tests/` changed, `tests/parity/compare.mjs`: the two classes the viewer's host carries for the look (`has-diff-lines`, `has-diff-body`) are not compared as state of the file (the unchanged script printed 1,272 notes for them, no regression). The two comparisons were run again on the sequence's dumps with the same listed differences and the same 88 (`r2/final/*.after-compare-change.txt`, `after-run-change.txt`).

**What still differs from main under `codeview`** (each either decided, or impossible to match with the reason and the measurement):

- *Context files* (decision D2, the look the user accepted): lines drawn by the viewer; gaps of five lines or fewer shown; the label "N unmodified lines"; large files uncoloured. Added by this round's measurement: 9 px of blank under a context file's last row, where main has none and the decision's screenshot had 8 (H127: the viewer's bottom padding is one number for every item, and a changed file's frame needs 9).
- *The scroll container* (plan 3.2): the viewer's root must be the scroll container, so the toolbar and the tour bar are above it, not pinned inside it. They are in the same pixels as on main at every position compared. The element that scrolls starts 59 px lower (115 px during a tour) and is that much shorter (`scroller.y`, `scroller.h` in the look check), so on a system that draws scrollbars permanently the scrollbar starts under the toolbar and not beside it. Headless Chromium draws no scrollbar: this one is stated from the element's box, not seen on a screenshot.
- *After a file's contents have loaded* (decision D5, correction 13; unchanged by this round): a changed file keeps git's hunks, so the lines main draws around each hunk after its re-diff are behind a separator ("9 unmodified lines" where main shows three more lines and "6 unmodified lines"). The look check holds the contents back for this reason; the golden comparison lists the 584 files times dumps.
- *Collapse while scrolled inside a file* (H111), *Local mode's empty diff* (H109), *the message when the viewer's bundle is missing* (decision D1): unchanged, as recorded by the first report.
- *Not compared yet, so not known to be equal*: the file-comment zone with a comment or a form in it (H125: inside the frame on `codeview`, above it on main; phase 6), a file whose diff is held back for its size ("Load diff"; phase 6 adds it to the look check), cards under lines (phase 6), hover and focus states.

Deviations from the plan in the fix round, with the proof:

- **The plan's layout numbers and the candidate CSS of correction 3 are replaced** (section 6, items 17 and 18): header 53 px; `layout` 17 / 41 / 24; `itemMetrics.paddingBottom: 9`; no `itemMetrics.paddingTop` (read in the vendor source: it would also apply to a file without lines); the scroll container has no box of its own.
- **`loadContextFiles` on `codeview` has a contract for the caller that awaits it** (section 6, item 19), which hazard H80 and plan 3.15.4 did not have.
- **Gates G9 and G13 test the CSS that ships** and load the pages' stylesheets; `CANDIDATE_CSS` is gone from the contract harness.
- **N5 changed in two more places.** One new scenario (above), with the named expectation `CONTEXT_LOADS_IN_FLIGHT` for what the default engine leaves in the pane. And "clicking a context entry in the sidebar brings its header to the top of the diff" compares the landing with what is pinned above the diff (`headerLanding`: within 2 px under the toolbar, or the diff at its end) where it compared with the first file's offset at the top of the pane: with the 24 px after the last file that main has, the viewer can bring the last context file's header all the way up, 0.3 px under the toolbar (main: 0 px), which the old comparison read as wrong (17 px expected). The same assertion on the default engine: 10 of 10.
- **The look check compares pixels with a tolerance** (H126), because the same glyph is rasterised differently in another compositing layer; positions are compared as numbers.
- **`vendor/context-items.spec.js` passes the three style blocks its facts are about**, not the bridge's whole `unsafeCSS()`: `FRAME_CSS` needs `itemMetrics.paddingBottom` with it, and gate G13 has the whole.

The verifier's non-blocking items: the fixed 300 ms wait of `layoutValidator.reportsAtRest` is now a rest condition (the pane has not moved from one frame to the next and every mounted file shows its published diff); an end-of-test check of the fixture that cannot run is reported, not counted as clean (a closed page apart); `resetLayoutCache` has its own contract test (it fails without the call); the comparison of hidden-line counts per engine rule and the two engine branches that do not read `_diffEngine` (`_setFileElementClass`, `_materializeDeferredDiff`) are recorded here for phases 8 and 9. Not changed: N5 "has a sidebar entry and nothing in the pane" still reads the scroll position two animation frames after the click; no sentinel on the same channel was found that would show a scroll that did not happen.

Carried forward, besides the first report's list: phase 6 owns H125 and adds a file-level comment, a "Load diff" file and a card under a line to `visual.mjs`; phase 7 owns the 88 landings and changed rows of the golden comparison and the tour's scroll to a stop in a file that is not mounted (the new N5 scenario brings the file into view itself); phase 9 repeats `visual.mjs` after the flip against the stored default-engine captures.


### Phase 6: cut-over 2 behind the switch, annotations and interaction

**State: built in five sessions; the fourth and the fifth each answer an independent verifier's rejection of the result before ("Session 4" and "Session 5" below).** Nothing is committed by either session (the verifier commits); evidence is under `evidence/phase-6/` of the session scratch directory (`HANDOFF.md` there is the running record). The first session's last serial sequence is `final/` (tree fingerprint `3954a8d549540a51`); the second session's is `final-s2/` (`4b7c18e9ac3b1509` at its start, between its stages and at its end), and its results are under "Session 2" below; the third session's is `final-s3/` ("Session 3"); the fourth's is `final-s4/` ("Session 4"); the fifth's is `final-s5/` ("Session 5"). What follows first is the first session's record as it was written, with its open list; "Session 2" says what became of each item.

#### Session 1

**Where the phase started.** Phase 3 had made every consumer decide from data and phase 4 had built the bridge's annotation API, so with the switch on most of the list already ran: 418 of 432 executed tests of the acceptance list passed on `codeview` before any change (`wip-accept-0.log`). The 14 that did not: the N5 tour scenario of phase 5's hand-off (both modes), the gutter characterization test (both modes), the document-wide summary count of N11 (both modes), and eight N11 tests that navigate to a file outside the mounted window.

**What was done.**

- *Gutter buttons after a scroll* (H129): `PierreCodeViewBridge._handleRootScroll`, `_watchScrollRest`, `_rederiveHoverAtRest`, `_tellPointer` (shared with the hover restore after an update). `GUTTER_BUTTON_STAYS_WITH_ANALYSIS_SUGGESTIONS` is `false` on `codeview`.
- *One draw per reload* (H11, H32): the old bridge has `batch(fn)` (it runs `fn`; every change is drawn by the file's own instance there), so consumers call it on both. `PRManager._loadUserComments` removes and adds the comment annotations of every file inside one `batch`, with no `await` in the block; `SuggestionManager._removeRenderedSuggestions` and `HunkSummaryRenderer.reset` remove inside one `batch`.
- *`pierreBridge.files.has` re-read against H83.* Every site in `pr.js`, `comment-manager.js`, `external-comment-manager.js`, `suggestion-manager.js`, `tour-renderer.js` and `ChatPanel.js` was read. One was wrong: `TourRenderer.prepareStop` asked the document for the stop's file, so on `codeview` a stop in a changed file that had no element called `ensureContextFile` (answered `{ type: 'diff' }`, harmless) and a stop in a context file that had no element could PATCH that file's stored range. It asks the bridge first now (`_isPierreFile(path)`, then the element, which on the default engine is the context wrapper). The content-upgrade paths answer for a context record without starting anything (its `baseMetadata` is set from the start); hunk-summary anchors are registered from the changed files only; `ChatPanel._scrollToLine` still returns early without an element, which is phase 7's (`CHAT_LINK_REACHES_UNRENDERED_FILE`).
- *Unit tests of the context-line paths* (`tests/unit/context-line-annotations.test.js`, the production `PRManager` methods on the production bridge with the recording viewer): a comment form on a context line is an annotation of the context item on the additions side with side `RIGHT` and a `null` diff position in its data; a stored `LEFT` comment is drawn on the item's one side and keeps `LEFT` in its data, which is the cache's own object; a reload, a suggestion clear and a summary reset are one forced render each; `ensureLinesVisible` on a context file adds a range and starts no content request. `tour-renderer.test.js` has the H83 case; `context-file-header.test.js` the summary toggle (H131).
- *H71 and H78* are the shared modules' and the bridge's since phases 3 and 4 and were not touched: a draw syncs transitions, a lookup never does (`bridge/annotations.spec.js`), and every path is resolved once at the bridge's entry. The third item of H71 (a suggestion whose adopting comment was deleted and restored turns "adopted" at the next draw, which on `codeview` is the next remount) is left as phase 4 decided it.
- *H52*: the suggestion block is byte-identical on both engines (`SUGGESTION_BLOCK_HAS_EMPTY_LINE`, `comment-forms.spec.js` asserts the exact text). *H45*: H130. *`BASE_CHANGE_KEEPS_CARDS`*: not flipped (H51).
- *The validator report of phase 5's hand-off* is vendor fact F27 (section 4), pinned in the vendor and the bridge contract suites, and the development-bundle fixture holds that one report for two frames (`tests/e2e/diff-dom-codeview.js`, `installInPage`). In the application the comparison is still unequal at the first frame's callbacks and equal at the second's, which is why it is two. The fixture's own rule is tested in `fixture-isolation.spec.js` by logging the viewer's message (the mechanism itself reports in about nine of ten executions in the application, so a self-test built on it was not deterministic; the contract suite has it deterministically).
- *E2E module*: `expectLineCardsInPane` (H135), `ensureMounted` for both engines (H134), the `codeview` versions of `expandHunkBy` (the viewer's own instance) and `redisplayBaseDiff` (the base diff published under a new key while the record's revealed ranges stay; the visibility oracle answers from what is published), which no spec of this phase's list calls.
- *The look check* (`tests/parity/visual.mjs`): summaries are enabled with one summary on the annotated file in every position, and four positions are added on that file: `cards` (a line comment, a comment on a range, suggestions open, dismissed, adopted and praise, a file-level comment and a file-level suggestion in the zone), `minimized`, `forms` (a form under a line and one in the zone, nothing focused) and `selection` (three lines, selected with the pointer on the line numbers). Per position the boxes of every card, form, summary and indicator relative to the file are compared as numbers (`measureCards`). It found H131, H132 and H133, which are fixed, and H125's extent, which is not.

#### Session 2

**What was open, and what became of each.**

- *H125, the zone's extent*: resolved (`ZONE_CSS`, hazards H125 and H136). The zone with a comment, a suggestion or a form in it is equal to main's in numbers and pixels in every configuration of the look check.
- *The two unexplained failures of the development-bundle run*: explained, each by a deterministic reproduction, and neither was a defect of the layout.
  - The `sticky container height` report that was "still unequal two frames later" is the review panel's width transition (H139). The fixture's rule now counts its two frames from when the pane's box last changed.
  - "The form is not visible after the click on the header's button" is a click the browser does not deliver when the file is drawn between the press and the release (H140). That one is a difference a reader can meet, and the bridge repairs it.
- *H119*: a guard is built (`_restoreTrimmedCards`; the entry has the mechanism, read in the vendor source, and the two tests).
- *The look check*: it has an external-thread position (PR mode), a comment in a context file's zone, and a third corpus, `lazy`, with a file whose diff is held back for its size; it runs both modes, both layouts and both themes for all three corpora. It found two more differences, fixed: the "Large diff ... Load diff" message was in the code font (slotted next to the zone, it inherited from the viewer's shadow tree; `pr.css` now sets for it what it sets for the zone), and a file without lines showed the frame's two side borders beside that message and beside the 1 px line of an empty diff (`FRAME_CSS`: transparent there). One position is decided and listed, not equal (H126, the end of the pane in split layout with the edge corpus).
- *The golden dump*: taken on both engines and compared (results below).

**What else changed.**

- `tests/parity/visual.mjs` waits for the animations of the document before it measures a card (H137), takes a number at the end of the pane, and can list a decided pixel difference (`where: 'pixels'`).
- The development-bundle fixture's failure message says what the pane was doing (how far off, which files, whether a file's diff was awaiting display), before the viewer's own object, because the console guard keeps 300 characters.
- `tests/e2e/fixture-isolation.spec.js`: two more self-tests of the fixture's rule per mode (a mismatch with the pane at rest reaches the guard; a report made while the review panel opens over a 4,000-line file is followed). They run in a development-bundle run only; a shipped-bundle run skips eight tests where it skipped four.
- `tests/CONVENTIONS.md`: two rules for the look check (measure after the animations; a difference one column wide can be inside the tolerance).

**Deviations from the plan in this session.**

- Plan 3.7 has the zone as an annotation and says nothing of its extent; the zone's row is stretched over the line-number column and the frame's borders by CSS in the viewer's shadow tree that names vendor markup (`ZONE_CSS`), the code block is no longer clipped, and cards are clipped one by one at their right edge.
- The bridge clicks an element itself when the browser withheld the click (H140), and draws a windowed file again when the viewer dropped a card (H119). Neither is in the plan.
- "Development-bundle run: validator 0" is now "no validator report that is still a mismatch two frames after the pane's box last changed" (H139).
- The look check lists one position as decided (H126).

Seven of the eight pass every later statement: the tour shows stop two on the tail and stop three back on the target, one stop row at a time, no toast, and leaving removes them, also from the file that is away; the navigator keys, the panel click and the review-history jump leave no toast and the card has the edit. The eighth, external threads, failed on its last count (2 for 3): a count over the document (H135). So the eight fail on navigation only, and what phase 6 owns in them (cards, stops and threads on a far file, once each, gone when they should be) holds.

#### Session 4

An independent verifier rejected the phase as the third session left it, with two findings. Both are fixed at their cause; the rest of the tree is the third session's.

- *A test of phase 5's list failed on `codeview`: "a very long line wraps: nothing scrolls sideways"* (`diff-render.spec.js`, both modes; 34 passed on phase 5's commit, 32 on the third session's tree). The phase's own list does not contain that spec and no sequence of the three sessions ran phase 5's list on `codeview`. What the test read is the unified code column lying over the frame's 1 px border, which is how the comment zone reaches the file's edges (H125); nothing can be scrolled. The CSS is unchanged and the measure is restated: a box that scrolls is asked how far, a box that does not is asked what sticks out past its border (H142, with what was measured, what was not done and why). The measure has a test of its own, in both modes and on both engines, that turns wrapping off in the page and expects it to read the line. `accept-list.sh` of the phase-6 evidence runs phase 5's list with the phase's own from here on.
- *The click repair of H140 did not work for the collapse chevron.* Every draw of a header re-stamps it, and the re-stamp writes the chevron's icon again, so the element that was pressed is not there at the release; the repair asked for the same element. It now finds the control: the innermost element the press was inside of, as it was when the press began, that is still in the file and has the release inside it (H140, fourth-session addendum). The tests that missed it used a stand-in header and one fixed element; the new ones drive the application's own header in a browser (`file-controls.spec.js`, four controls, both modes, both engines) and on the production bridge (`tests/unit/header-click-repair.test.js`), and the contract test has a control that is re-stamped on every draw.

- *Found by this session's own whole-suite run on `codeview`:* `pr-page.spec.js` "should expand context and show correct line numbers with offset" failed once. It is a test of main's that clicked an expand control while the diff could still be drawing itself; the click is lost to a draw on both engines (H143: measured on both, 1 of 120 on the default engine under load). The test waits for the diff to settle now; the behaviour is main's and is not changed.

**What changed in this session.** `public/js/modules/pierre-codeview-bridge.js` (`_handleRootPointerDown`, `_pressedControl`, `_handlePointerUp`); `tests/e2e/diff-dom.js` (`wrapInfo`, `setLineLayout`, `redrawFile`), `tests/e2e/diff-dom-codeview.js` (`redrawFile`), `tests/e2e/diff-render.spec.js` (one test per mode), `tests/e2e/file-controls.spec.js` (four tests per mode), `tests/e2e/pr-page.spec.js` (the wait of H143), `tests/contract/bridge/bridge-page.js` (`BH.onHeaderDraw`), `tests/contract/bridge/gutter.spec.js`, `tests/unit/pierre-codeview-bridge.test.js` (three tests), `tests/unit/header-click-repair.test.js` (new, six tests), `tests/CONVENTIONS.md` (three rules). No CSS, nothing of the default engine.

**Deviations from the plan in this session.**

- The repair's rule (H140): a press and a release on different parts of a file's header, zone or card with a draw in between is clicked on what both are in, which is what a browser does without the draw.
- `wrapInfo` no longer counts what lies over a non-scrolling box's own border (H142). On the default engine its numbers are unchanged.

#### Session 5

The verifier rejected the fourth session's result with one finding. It is fixed at its cause; apart from it, and one race in five tests of the contract suite that this session's first sequence ran into, the tree is the fourth session's.

- *The click repair of H140 delivered a tap twice.* A tap on a header control with a draw of the file between touch start and touch end was clicked by the bridge at the end of the release's task and by the browser a few milliseconds later, so a tapped chevron or Viewed checkbox ended where it began (both modes; the default engine gets one click). The repair assumed what holds for a mouse: that no click by the end of the release's task means the browser withheld it. A browser makes the click of a tap in another way, after the release, for whatever is under the point then, and a draw does not take it away. Measured with a mouse, a pen and a touch before anything was changed (H140, fifth-session addendum): the click is withheld for a mouse and for a pen the browser handles as one, and delivered for a touch. The bridge now repairs only a press the browser handles as a mouse press, and reads which kind a press is from the event the browser sends with it (`mousedown` or `touchstart`), with the pointer type recorded at the press as the answer when neither came (`_notePressKind`, `_isMousePress`). The mouse path is unchanged.
- *Why three sessions did not see it:* no test used anything but a mouse. The E2E block now runs each of its tests with a mouse, a pen and a touch, counts the clicks that reach the document, and starts each test with a plain gesture, because the first tap of a page hides the defect (its click comes in the task of the release). `tests/CONVENTIONS.md` has the rule.
- *Found by this session's first sequence:* the contract suite failed once, in `gates/g10-pending-swap-interleaving.spec.js` "a second replacement issued before the first is displayed" (`TypeError: Cannot read properties of null (reading 'tokens')` in the test's own bring-up; 231 of 231 in every earlier sequence). The test waited with `H.until(() => H.shape(T).tokens > 0)` right after `H.appLike({ pool: true })`. `H.shape` answers `null` for a file with no element; with the worker pool nothing is mounted until its workers are up (G7); and `H.appLike` returns after 150 ms without a viewer event, which under load is before that. Five tests had that statement (`g10`, `g12`, `vendor/internals`, `vendor/swap`, `vendor/scroll`); each now reads "no element" as "not yet", the way the bridge specs already did. The condition waited for is the same. Those five spec files `--repeat-each=6`: 144 of 144 (`s5/contract-shape-wait-repeat6.log`; the failing run is `s5/contract-g10-flake-seen.log`).

**What changed in this session.** `public/js/modules/pierre-codeview-bridge.js` (the press record, `_notePressKind`, `_isMousePress`, one condition in `_handlePointerUp`, two listeners on the pane's root and their removal); `tests/e2e/file-controls.spec.js` (the block of H140: five tests with each of three pointers per mode, and its helpers `watchClicks`, `gesture`, `clicksOfPressDrawRelease`); `tests/contract/bridge/gutter.spec.js` (`clickTargets` taken out of the mouse test; "a press that is not a mouse button", four tests); `tests/unit/pierre-codeview-bridge.test.js` (seven tests), `tests/unit/header-click-repair.test.js` (one test); the five contract spec files of the race above (one statement each); `tests/CONVENTIONS.md` (one rule). No CSS, nothing that draws, nothing of the default engine.

**Deviations from the plan in this session.**

- The bridge's click repair (H140, not in the plan) applies to a mouse press only; a tap gets the browser's own click, as on the default engine. The fourth session's statement that a control pressed around a draw "acts once" was true for a mouse and false for a touch; it holds for a mouse, a pen and a touch now, and is tested with each.
- Not measured, and said so in H140: a pen that a platform reports the way it reports a touch. The test browser cannot produce one; by the rule it is treated as a touch.

### Phase 7: cut-over 3 behind the switch, reveal, expansion, navigation

Started from `f6cf38d7`. With the switch on, everything that brings a place of the diff into view works on the virtualized viewer for a target the viewer has not mounted; a file's contents load into the same rows as on main; and the whole E2E suite passes on `codeview`.

**What was built.**

- *Navigation to what is not mounted* (`PRManager.revealInDiff`, steps 3 and 4; correction 21). A card the viewer does not draw is brought into its mounted window with an instant line target (`PierreCodeViewBridge.scrollToAnnotation`; an item target and `whenElementDrawn` for a card in a file's comment zone), the helper waits for it as before, and the landing is main's own `scrollIntoViewStable`. "Drawn" is asked of the bridge (`isElementDrawn`), not of `isConnected`: second session, H154. The eight navigation tests of N11 and the three AI-panel tests of N4 pass with nothing else changed in them.
- *Two navigations from one key press* (H144): the one that started last wins, and the pane is not moved for an unmounted target before every handler of the event has started. `SuggestionNavigator` does not report "Could not reveal this finding" for a navigation that another one took over.
- *Chat links* (`ChatPanel._handleFileLinkClick`, `_scrollToLine`; H51): decided from the bridge's record of the path and from `isLineVisible`, not from wrappers and chunks in the DOM; the line goes through `revealInDiff` as a line target (`_revealLines`: go to the line if the file is not mounted, wait until the line is what the file draws, scroll unless it is on screen, flash the range). The bridge's flash holds a range of lines (`scrollToLine` with `endLine`), and `ifNeeded` keeps main's "no scroll when the line is on screen". `CHAT_LINK_REACHES_UNRENDERED_FILE` and `REVEALED_LINE_KEEPS_FLASH` are `true` on `codeview`. `ensureContextFile` answers without a request for a line a context item shows without a stored range (H150).
- *The content upgrade* (decision D5a, correction 20): a re-diff of the two contents with the patch's lines kept, as on main. `expansion.spec.js` "an expand control reveals a step ..." passes unchanged; `UPGRADED_DIFF_HUNKS` is removed.
- *The typing gate* for a background upgrade (correction 22): `UPGRADE_WAITS_FOR_FOCUS` is `true` on `codeview`.
- *Manual expansions through a fold* (H120, H146, fact F28): the lines a reader expanded stay on screen while a reveal elsewhere in the file awaits display, whichever way the reveal comes (`addContextRanges`, a content upgrade, another stored range of a context file through `reconcile`; the last since the second session).
- *Collapsing the file the reader is inside* (H111, H145): the collapsed header stays flush under the toolbar; `COLLAPSE_INSIDE_FILE`.
- *A tour's stop lands where it lands on main* (H152).
- *A navigation holds list writes nobody asked for* (`bridge.holdListWrites`, plan 3.4) until its final scroll has come to rest. A line target lets go when the viewer's own scroll has been asked for, not when it has ended: a context file pushed in the following fraction of a second would cut that scroll short and leave the line flashed where the list write puts the view.

**Carried from the verifiers of phases 5 and 6, each closed.**

- The two gaps of the click repair (H140): a press that opens the context menu, and two presses with no pause. Tests in the E2E block on both engines, unit tests, a mutation run.
- `_clearDiffItems` drops `_contextLoadTail`: two loads in flight when Local mode's empty-diff path runs no longer put a context file back into the emptied pane. Two unit tests that hold two loads (both fail with the line removed).
- N5 "has a sidebar entry and nothing in the pane": the two-frame window is a sentinel (the same click on the entry that has somewhere to go, and the page's requests to scroll the pane by then: `watchScrollRequests` / `scrollRequests`).
- N5's two `ensureMounted` calls are removed (H134).
- The three `setTimeout(resolve, 30)` waits of `bridge/gutter.spec.js` are a click on a sentinel element (`clicksBehindSentinel`); the 5 ms ticks of the two unit files are 0 ms timers set after the bridge's own, which run after it.
- The `panel-resize.spec.js:115` flake is explained and settled at its cause (H148).

**Named expectations after this phase** (H51): on `codeview`, `CHAT_LINK_REACHES_UNRENDERED_FILE`, `REVEALED_LINE_KEEPS_FLASH` and `UPGRADE_WAITS_FOR_FOCUS` are `true` and `COLLAPSE_INSIDE_FILE` is `'header'`; `UPGRADED_DIFF_HUNKS` no longer exists. The default engine has main's value for each.

**Tests that state the default engine's behaviour only.** None is skipped on `codeview`. Four branches of specs assert main's behaviour under a named expectation and the virtualized viewer's under its other value: `scroll-landing.spec.js` "collapsing the file being read: what follows moves up into view" (`COLLAPSE_INSIDE_FILE`), `chat-file-links.spec.js` "a link to a line in a file that has never been on screen" and "a link to a line hidden between two hunks ..." (the two chat-link expectations), and `expansion.spec.js` "a draft typed before the upgrade is still in the form after it" (`UPGRADE_WAITS_FOR_FOCUS`). Phase 8 drops the default branch of each.

**One E2E title changed:** `scroll-landing.spec.js` "collapsing the file being read keeps the scroll position; what follows moves up into view" is now "collapsing the file being read: what follows moves up into view" (the first half is true of one engine only). **Titles added:** `file-controls.spec.js` "two presses on the chevron with no pause between them, the file drawn in each: two clicks, and the file is as it was" and "a press that opens the context menu acts as it does without a draw: Control held, and the secondary button" (with a mouse, both modes); `fixture-isolation.spec.js` "the interruption the top-level loader logs does not fail a test" (both modes). None removed.

**What changed, by file.** `public/js/pr.js` (`revealInDiff`, `_revealSteps`, `_revealLines`, `_afterInputHandlers`, `_waitForPierrePointerIdle`, `ensureContextFile`, `_clearDiffItems`); `public/js/modules/pierre-codeview-bridge.js` (`upgradeStrategy`, `patchParityRanges`, `_rediff`, `parityRanges` in `_derive`; `scrollToLine` options, `scrollToAnnotation`, `isElementDrawn`, `whenElementDrawn`, `_displayWillDraw`, `_isRowInView`, `_flashLines`; `setCollapsed` and `_isReaderInside`; `_retireExpansions`, `_drawsNextDiffAtOnce`, `_watchForNextDiff`, `_unwatchNextDiff`, `_forgetInstance`, `_dropRetiredExpansions`, `_applyContextEntry`; `isEditingInside`; `_onRootClick`, `_onRootContextMenu`); `public/js/components/ChatPanel.js`, `public/js/components/SuggestionNavigator.js`; `public/css/pr.css` (the tour stop's scroll margin, inside the `codeview` block). Not touched: `AIPanel.js`, `scroll-into-view.js` and `_materializeDeferredDiff`, which the plan lists for this phase: the first two already went through `revealInDiff` and needed nothing for an unmounted target, and the third has been an `updateItem` on the same id since phase 5 (`bridge.materializeDeferred`). `tests/e2e`: `diff-dom.js`, `diff-dom-codeview.js`, `console-guard.js`, `scroll-landing.spec.js`, `context-files.spec.js`, `file-controls.spec.js`, `diff-render.spec.js`, `fixture-isolation.spec.js`, `offscreen-mutations.spec.js` (second session). `tests/parity`: `visual.mjs` (the second page), `compare.mjs` (the post-upgrade stage must be identical; the decided landing is listed). `tests/contract`: `bridge/contents.spec.js`, `bridge/context-records.spec.js`, `bridge/annotations.spec.js` and `vendor/internals.spec.js` (second session), `bridge/list-write-in-callback.spec.js`, `bridge/gutter.spec.js`, `gates/g12-reveal-latency-current-engine.spec.js`. `tests/unit`: `pierre-codeview-bridge.test.js`, `reveal-in-diff.test.js`, `chat-panel.test.js`, `SuggestionNavigator.test.js`, `load-context-files.test.js`, `pierre-render-budget.test.js`, `engine-switch-sites.test.js`, `e2e-console-guard.test.js`, `header-click-repair.test.js`, and the new `ensure-context-file.test.js`. `tests/CONVENTIONS.md` (four rules).

**The look check, extended** (`tests/parity/visual.mjs`, parity row 100). A second page per configuration lets the contents arrive and compares numbers and pixels of: every file drawn from its contents, the top and the end of the pane, a gap expanded by one step and in full, a line revealed by a navigation, a flashed line (the animation stopped 500 ms in, on both engines), and five landings (sidebar click, chat link, a tour's first stop, a click on a finding, the same with the finding's file collapsed). Its first run found the tour's landing 57 px lower on `codeview` (H152, fixed).

**Deviations from the plan in this phase.**

- Decision D5 is superseded by D5a (the orchestrator's decision on H118): the upgrade is main's re-diff. The plan's "landing positions within 2 px of the phase-2 goldens" is met through the golden comparison between the engines on one tree, which is stricter (1 px, and the same corpus state).
- `revealInDiff` (correction 21): a line target does not open a collapsed file and is served on `codeview` only; `scrollToContextFile` is not a caller; the latest navigation wins.
- The focus gate is a typing gate (correction 22).
- Collapsing the file the reader is inside keeps its header in view, where main loses it (H145). Deliberate and visible.
- Kept from main although the plan's helper would have changed it: a chat link to a line of a collapsed file goes to the file's header and leaves the file collapsed; `j` and `k` are answered by two components with two cursors (H144).
- Not built: restoring the keyboard focus after a reveal the reader asked for lands in the file they are typing in (the typing gate covers background upgrades only; main loses the focus there too).
- H119's separator half (a file whose row count is an exact multiple of 5,000) stays for phase 9, and H143 (an expand-control click lost to a draw, on both engines) and H147(c) (whitespace changes shown again after the upgrade with "Hide whitespace" on, read in the code, on both engines) are candidates for the defects issue (#638).

**What a user can see differently from main after this phase, on `codeview`** (the list the orchestrator asked for; everything else compared is equal).

1. Collapsing a file while scrolled inside it: the collapsed file's header stays at the top of the pane with the next file under it. On main nothing scrolls, the header is left above the viewport and a later file is on screen (H145).
2. A jump to a card or a line in a file far away (review panel, navigator keys, tour, chat link, review history): the pane goes to the target at once and then settles on it with main's own short smooth scroll. On main the whole distance is scrolled smoothly, and the landing is corrected afterwards when files on the way have rendered.
3. When the contents of the file being read arrive, the line at the top of the pane stays where it is and the pane's scroll position grows by the rows added above it. On main the line moves down by those rows (H151). The rows themselves are the same.
4. A chat link to a line of a file that has never been near the viewport arrives and flashes the line; a line that had to be revealed keeps its flash for the full three and a half seconds; a range is flashed as one. On main the first does not arrive and the second flashes for a frame or two (H51).
5. A file's contents do not load into it while the reader is typing in one of its comment forms; they load when the focus leaves the field. On main they load, and the form loses the focus (H51).
6. Lines expanded by hand stay on screen while a later reveal in the same file is prepared, as on main (they used to leave the screen for a moment on `codeview`, H120). This holds for every way a reveal comes, including another range of a context file from a chat link, a tour stop or a push (second session).
7. A card a navigation lands on can be up to 0.7 px higher or lower than on main in a diff with wrapped lines (H153).

**Flakes.** None in the five whole-suite runs of the final sequence (three on `codeview` with the shipped bundle, one with the development bundle, one on the default engine), nor in the repeats listed below. `panel-resize.spec.js:115`, seen once in the phase-6 verification, is explained and cannot fail a test that way any more (H148). During the phase one race of a test helper was found and removed (H149), and one of the dump tool (H151).

**Second session: the verifier's two findings, each fixed at its cause.** The first session's report was rejected by the phase's verifier (round 1) on two points that its green suites did not reach. The table above is that session's record; the results that count are in the table below.

1. *Navigation did nothing for a card on a row the viewer does not draw* (a regression against main, both modes; H154, F29, correction 24). The viewer attaches the card of every annotation of a mounted file, drawn row or not, and the first session took "connected" for "drawn" in three places. Fixed: `PierreCodeViewBridge.isElementDrawn` asks the slot the card is shown through; `scrollToAnnotation`, `whenAnnotationRendered` and the new `whenElementDrawn` (in place of `whenFileMounted`) decide from it, and so do step 3 of `revealInDiff` and its comment-zone branch. A card that is attached while its file's new diff awaits display is still handed to the scroll at once, as on main (H154, `_displayWillDraw`). The verifier's probe after the fix (`s2/probe-huge-window-codeview-after.log`, a 9,000-line file): the panel click lands at scroll 139,739 (default engine 139,737), the second `j` on the far suggestion, the comment click at 143,389 (143,389), in both modes; before, nothing moved.
2. *A range added to a context file after a hand expansion: the expanded lines left the screen and the development validator reported 382 px* (H120, H146, F28). `_applyContextEntry`'s fold emptied the viewer's map at once. Fixed: the fold moves the lines into the record's ranges and nothing else; all three paths that derive a mounted record again retire the map after the derive, and the row renderer is asked whether the next draw is the new diff (then the map goes before the publish) or the one on screen (then it is kept and dropped when the new diff's result is staged). The verifier's probe after the fix (`s2/probe-context-fold-codeview-after.log`, `-dev-after.log`): no frame without the row of line 130 in either mode, no console line with the development bundle. The bridge's `_clearExpansions`, an alias that only forwarded to `_retireExpansions`, is gone (earlier entries of this ledger name it). The same change closes a case the verifier's contract probe showed and did not list: a reveal in a file drawn as plain text reported `delta: 80` once (the fallback path); it now reports nothing (`s2/probe-contract-nav-bridge-after.log`).

*Tests added, and what each fails on.* E2E, both modes and both engines: N11 "Navigation to a card far down a file longer than the rows the viewer draws" ("a finding and a comment thousands of lines down are reached from the review panel and with the navigator keys"; "from far down the long file, a file-level comment and a card near its top are reached from the review panel"), on an added file of 12,000 lines; N5 "a range added to a file after the reader expanded a gap in it: the expanded lines stay on screen throughout", with a frame-by-frame watch (`watchContextLines` / `contextLinesWatched` in `diff-dom.js`). Contract (real bundle, validator on): `bridge/annotations.spec.js` "a card on a row the viewer does not draw is in the document and not drawn; scrollToAnnotation and whenAnnotationRendered bring it on screen" (20,000 lines; pins F29) and "a card on a line that was just revealed: its file's display draws it where the pane is, unless the file is drawn in row windows, where the pane is taken to it first" (a 400-line and a 7,000-line coloured context file); `vendor/internals.spec.js` "hunksRenderer.getDiffForNextRender names the diff the next draw will be, and onRenderUpdate is called when an awaited diff has its result staged, before it is drawn" (F28's internals, without the bridge); `bridge/context-records.spec.js` "a stored range added to a context file / an uncoloured context file after the reader expanded a gap ..."; `bridge/contents.spec.js` "a reveal in a file drawn as plain text, after the reader expanded a gap ...". Unit: `pierre-codeview-bridge.test.js` (`isElementDrawn` on a real shadow tree with slots, `scrollToAnnotation` and `whenAnnotationRendered` for a connected card without a slot, `whenElementDrawn`; the describe "a context file given another range after the reader expanded a gap by hand"; the at-once draw; the renderer's callback given back on every path), `reveal-in-diff.test.js` (a card in the document and not drawn; never drawn; attached while its file awaits display; a zone card of a long file, drawn and never drawn). Two unit tests of `whenFileMounted` went with the method. Mutation runs, each with the fix taken out and nothing else changed: "drawn" meaning "connected" again, the four new N11 tests fail on `codeview` (`s2/mutation-drawn-is-connected-codeview.log`); the comment-zone branch alone on `isConnected`, the two zone tests fail (`s2/mutation-zone-connected-codeview.log`); the fold emptying the map, the N5 test fails in both modes with 7 and 11 frames without the rows (`s2/mutation-fold-clears-e2e-codeview.log`), the coloured contract test fails with the validator's lines and four unit tests fail (`s2/mutation-fold-clears-*.log`); the renderer never asked, the two plain-text contract tests fail with `delta: 80` (`s2/mutation-never-at-once-contract.log`); a windowed file's display trusted to draw the card, the "just revealed" contract test fails (`s2/mutation-display-trusted-in-windows-contract.log`).

*The sequence was started twice.* Its first start (`s2/final-aborted-1/`: unit 11,845, contract 240, then three whole-suite runs on `codeview`, each 906 passed and the same 2 failed) was stopped after the third run. The two failures were `inline-feedback-context.spec.js` "suggestions spanning a visible hunk and hidden endpoint render and navigate" in both modes: first a helper of `diff-dom-codeview.js` (`redisplayBaseDiff`) that called a private method of the bridge this session had renamed, and behind that the real finding, the pending-display state described in H154, which the first form of the fix had changed. Both were corrected, the affected specs and the verifier's probes were run again, and the sequence below ran from its beginning on the corrected tree.

*Measured, not assumed.* The row blocks of F29 (`s2/probe-contract-window.log`). The landing of each navigation of the two new N11 tests, traced for four seconds after the click on both engines: the card's top comes to rest 176.5 px from the top of the page on both, at every step, in both modes (`s2/probe-landing-trace-codeview.log`, `-filediff.log`). The new N11 tests take 9.0 to 9.7 s on the default engine alone and under an eight-worker load (`s2/wip-load-filediff.log`) and 2.1 to 2.8 s on `codeview`: no `test.slow()` (H77).

*No change of what a user sees* beyond the two defects being gone; item 6 of the list above now holds on every path. No named expectation changed. No test was removed or loosened; one assertion written in this session was withdrawn before it was part of any run that counts (the labels of a context file's gap rows after a hand expansion and a second range read "100 hidden lines" and "199 hidden lines" on the default engine's table renderer, a defect of main that leaves with that renderer in phase 8; the test asserts the ranges and the one separator, which both engines agree on).

### Phase 8: flip to the virtualized viewer; the per-file renderer, the switch and the table renderer deleted

Three sessions (the first two ended when the account ran out of credits; the first had made the changes under `public/` and to the parity scripts, the third ran everything for the first time, repaired the unit tests and added what the phase-7 verification asked for).

**What the application is now.** One renderer of the diff pane: `PierreBridge` (`public/js/modules/pierre-bridge.js`, the file and class that were `pierre-codeview-bridge.js` / `PierreCodeViewBridge`) over one `CodeView`. `PRManager` builds it in `_createBridge`; nothing reads an engine name. Every line of a changed file and of a context file is drawn by the vendor inside an item of that viewer. Gone:

- *The switch:* `window.__PAIR_REVIEW_DIFF_ENGINE`, `PRManager._diffEngine`, `PR_ENGINE` in the E2E fixture and the parity scripts, the `.codeview-engine` class and CSS scope.
- *The per-file renderer:* the old `pierre-bridge.js` (2,301 lines), and in `pr.js` the lazy bodies (`ensureFileBodyRendered`, `_lazyFileBodies`, the body observer), `renderFileDiff`, `_renderFileBodyNow` / `_renderPierreFileBodyNow`, the total highlight budget (`_createPierreRenderBudget`), `_measureFileHeaderHeight`, `_setFileElementClass`, `_removeRenderedFiles`, the scroll restores around a layout switch.
- *The table renderer of context files* and everything only it used: `renderPatch`, `parseBlockLines`, `renderDiffLine`, the gap code (`expandGapContext`, `expandGapRange`, `validatePendingEofGaps`, `expandForSuggestion`), `renderContextFile` and its chunk builders, `findContextFileWrapper`, the legacy hunk anchors (`_registerHunkAnchorsForFile`, the `<tr>` branch of `_renderOneSummary`); `line-tracker.js` and `gap-coordinates.js`; the gap half of `hunk-parser.js`; `DiffRenderer.renderDiffLine` with highlight.js and its language map; every row-based method of `comment-manager.js` (`showCommentForm`, `saveUserComment`, `displayUserComment`, `displayUserCommentInEditMode`, `displaySuggestionEditForm`); the row insertion of `suggestion-manager.js`; the `<tr>` path of `external-comment-manager.js`; `TourRenderer._mountStopLegacy`; `HunkSummaryRenderer.renderInline`; `ChatPanel._findLineRows` and the DOM branch of its file links; the document fallbacks of `AIPanel`'s finding and comment navigation; the highlight.js tags of `pr.html` and `local.html` (the diff pages ask cdnjs for nothing; N2 has a test with that host aborted).
- `pr.js` went from 11,247 lines at the phase-7 commit to 8,845 (main: 9,657); `pr.css` from 14,767 to 14,131 (main: 14,544); 132 files changed, 6,779 lines added, 23,829 removed against the phase-7 commit.

**Shared functions that changed or went, and their callers** (the repository's rule; `evidence/phase-8/callers-at-phase7.txt` and `callers-now.txt` are the two lists, made by one script over `public/js`):

| Function | Change | Callers now |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `renderDiff` | the former `_renderDiffCodeView`; no engine branch | `pr.js` (`loadAndDisplayFiles`), `local.js` (`loadLocalDiff`) |
| `loadContextFiles` | the former `_loadContextFilesCodeView`; option `rerenderFile` gone | `pr.js`: `renderDiff`, the `context_files_changed` listener, `removeContextFile`, `ensureContextFile` (after a PATCH and after a POST), the visibility handler, `_afterOvertakenContextLoad` |
| `_clearDiffItems` | also releases the comment zones (it replaces `_removeRenderedFiles`) | `local.js` (`loadLocalDiff`, a scope without changes) |
| `showCommentForm(fileName, lineNumber, diffPosition, endLineNumber, side)` | no target row | `pr.js`: the gutter's comment button, for a line and for a selected range (two calls in `_createBridge`'s `onCommentClick`) |
| `collapseSuggestionForAdoption(suggestionId)` | resolves the card itself (`findSuggestionCard`) | `pr.js`: `_adoptAndBuildComment`, `editAndAdoptSuggestion`'s submit |
| `_renderAdoptedUserComment(comment)` | no suggestion row; an annotation of the viewer | `pr.js`: `editAndAdoptSuggestion`'s submit, `adoptSuggestion` |
| `getFileAndLineInfo(card)` | from the card's stored data only; no `targetRow` / `suggestionRow` | `pr.js`: `_adoptAndBuildComment`, `editAndAdoptSuggestion` (through the delegate `PRManager.getFileAndLineInfo`) |
| `setFileCollapsed` | no body render before the writes, so no `await` before them and no superseding of a waiting request (`_collapseRequests` gone); the element's class is the viewer's | `pr.js`: `toggleFileCollapse`, `toggleFileViewed` (two), `revealInDiff`, the context toggles (two) |
| `revealInDiff` | no `handled`, no `virtualized` branch, no lazy-body step; `findElement` kept (correction 27) | `AIPanel` (three), `ChatPanel._scrollToLine`, `SuggestionNavigator.goToSuggestion`, `TourRenderer` |
| `findCommentCard`, `findSuggestionCard` | no document fallback (H156) | `pr.js` (nine and seven call sites), `utils/posted-comment.js`, `utils/suggestion-ui.js`, `SuggestionNavigator` |
| `handleDiffViewChange` | returns what `setDiffStyle` answers; restores no scroll position | `pr.js` and `local.js` (the diff options) |
| `ensureLinesVisible` | data only (the bridge's oracle and `addContextRanges`) | `pr.js` (four), `suggestion-manager.js`, `external-comment-manager.js`, `tour-renderer.js` |
| `scrollIntoViewStable` | no render-first step | `pr.js` (`revealInDiff`), `tour-renderer.js` |
| `ensureContextFile` | a range is asked line by line (H150) | `ChatPanel._handleFileLinkClick`, `TourRenderer` (a stop in a file that is not in the pane) |
| `hideCommentForm` (`pr.js`, `comment-manager.js`), `displayUserComment` / `saveUserComment` (`comment-manager.js`), `renderInline`, `_resolveRow`'s legacy half, `getLineNumber`, `detectLanguage`, `extractFunctionContext`, `createGapRowElement` and the other gap builders | deleted with their only callers (the table renderer's paths) | none (`FileCommentManager` has methods of the same names for its zones; they are its own) |

**CSS.** 190 selectors of the table renderer and of the per-file renderer's body were dropped from `pr.css` and `styles.css`, five keyframes nothing names any more, and the `.codeview-engine` scope of 19 rules (all of which name `.diff-view`). How their removal was checked: (1) every class of every dropped selector was searched in `public/**/*.js`, `public/*.html` and `src/`; none is written by any code any more, on any page (the only classes of dropped selectors that are still in use, `.d2h-file-wrapper` and `.collapsed`, stood in compound selectors with a dropped class); (2) a rule-level comparison of both stylesheets with the phase-7 commit shows no rule changed but the three that were merged with their scoped twin (`.main-layout .diff-view`, `.d2h-file-header`, `.diff-view .pr-file-header-slot > .d2h-file-header`) and three selector lists that lost a row-level member; (3) the pages that share `pr.css` without being diff pages (`settings.html`, `repo-settings.html`; `index.html` and `setup.html` do not load it) contain none of `diff-view`, `diff-container`, `d2h-`, `main-layout`; (4) the look check below, in every layout, theme and mode of its matrix. No dropped selector had to be restored. *What the look check did find* is the un-scoping itself: with one class fewer, one of the viewer's rules lost to a general rule and every file with a file-level comment was a pixel taller, with a second line under its comment zone (H160). The sixteen selectors of the block now start with `.main-layout .diff-view`, the weight they had, and an E2E test in both modes holds the zone's lines. Two more rules went in the third session: `body:not(.tour-active) .d2h-file-wrapper tr` (a member of the scroll-margin list) and `.hunk-info` ("legacy support"; nothing writes the class).

**What the phase-7 verification asked for.** (a) A retired expansion map is dropped when its file is unmounted (H146, F28; contract test, with a mutation run). (b) Correction 21(d) and H146's "what is still lost" are corrected; the misdirected expansion is a candidate for #638 and is not fixed. (c) `ensureContextFile` asks every line of a range (H150; two unit tests). (d) `_displayWillDraw` asks the pending diff too (H155). As first built this was proven on a recording stand-in only and did not work against the real viewer; the phase's verifier rejected it, and the fix round below is what holds now. (e) `contents.spec.js` calls `PierreBridge.patchParityRanges`. From phase 5's verification: `_clearDiffItems` drops `_contextLoadTail` (done in phase 5's fix round; three unit tests in `load-context-files.test.js` hold it, "two loads in flight when the diff is emptied ..." among them).

**Parity against the old engine after it is gone.** `tests/parity/reference.mjs` runs a second checkout's own parity scripts and saves their output; `visual.mjs` takes `--reference <dir>` or `--reference-checkout <path>`, `perf.mjs` takes `--reference <file>`, `compare.mjs` tells the two sides apart by what a dump says of itself. The reference set is `evidence/phase-8/reference/` (golden dumps of 32 configurations, geometry and 504 screenshots, performance medians; `REFERENCE.json`), taken from a `git worktree` of the phase-7 commit (H159). *Before the deletion* the reworked scripts were run from a second worktree of the phase-7 commit with the switch on, against that reference: golden "no regression" with the same 16 listed differences as phase 7's own comparison; look check exit 0 with the same counts (480 pairs, 400 equal, 72 not compared, 1,128 number differences, all accepted, the 44 accepted groups the same value for value); the reworked `compare.mjs` on phase 7's stored dumps gave a byte-identical report (`evidence/phase-8/before/`, `compare-rework-on-phase7-dumps.txt`). *After the deletion:* the table below.

**Tests.** Unit: the test files of deleted code are deleted (`lazy-*`, `measure-file-header-height`, `expand-for-suggestion`, `gap-expansion-endlinenew`, `file-start-gap-header`, `render-patch-hunk-hashes`, `hljs-language-map`, `comment-manager-suggestion-edit`, the eight `pierre-bridge-*` files of the old bridge, `engine-switch-sites`); `pierre-codeview-bridge.test.js` is `pierre-bridge.test.js`; the cases of row-based functions are removed from the files plan 7.2 names and what they protected is asserted on the viewer's annotations instead (H156). New: `single-diff-viewer.test.js` (correction 29). E2E: `diff-dom-filediff.js` is deleted, `diff-dom.js` has one adapter and no named expectation with two values, the fixture has no engine variable; N2 gained the cdnjs test. Contract: the bridge specs load `pierre-bridge.js`; gate G12 measures the bridge alone; one new bridge test and one bundle test (H158).

What the first two attempts at this sequence found, both fixed before the results above:

1. *First sequence* (`final-run1-look-check-failed/`): everything passed (three whole-suite runs of 910 included) except the look check, which failed on every file with a filled comment zone. The cause is H160; nothing else in the tree could have shown it. The look-check script's own call of `showCommentForm` still had the old argument order, so its "forms" state had no line form; corrected in the same step.
2. *Second sequence* (`final/`, the tree as it is; its unit, contract, golden, look-check and performance results are the ones in the table): the second of its three whole-suite runs had one failure, `offscreen-mutations.spec.js` "a comment saved while a comment reload is in flight ..." in Local mode. It is a race in the E2E helper that opens a comment form, present at the phase-7 commit as well (H161). The helper was corrected (once more after its first correction closed a legitimate range form) and the E2E part was run again on its own (`final-e2e/`: unit, three whole-suite runs, the development-bundle run). Between `final/` and `final-e2e/` only `tests/e2e/diff-dom.js` changed; the fingerprint of everything under `public/` and `scripts/` is the same in both (`final/fingerprint-public-now.txt`, `final-e2e/fingerprint-public.txt`).

Named expectations left in `diff-dom.js` are not engine differences: `SUGGESTION_BLOCK_HAS_EMPTY_LINE` (H52) and `BASE_CHANGE_KEEPS_CARDS` (H1) name defects of main that are characterized and kept, `SMALL_CONTEXT_GAP_LINES` and `contextGapLabel` the viewer's rule for a context file's gaps (D2), `WORKER_LOAD_FAILURE_ERROR` the console line of a worker that cannot load.

**README.** No change: it describes neither how the diff is drawn, nor large-PR behaviour, nor context files' look, nor highlight.js. The changeset is `.changeset/virtualized-code-view.md` (`minor`).

**Files over the size guideline that this phase touched** (about 20K tokens; none was split here): `pr.js` (351 KB; 462 KB before the phase), `pr.css` (331 KB), `ChatPanel.js` (258 KB), `pierre-bridge.js` (144 KB; 155 KB after the fix round), `AIPanel.js` (105 KB), and of the tests `chat-panel.test.js` (386 KB) and `pierre-bridge.test.js` (133 KB; 148 KB after the fix round).

#### Fix round after verification round 1

**The finding.** The verifier ran the H155 change against the real bundle (`evidence/phase-8/verify/`: `verdict.json`, `probe-h155-many-files.log`, the probe specs under `probes/`): a reveal that takes a file drawn whole past 5,000 rows left the revealed line and its card undrawn, the navigation ran into its four-second limit, and with files after the long one the validator reported the sticky container 160 px short, a report the code before the change did not produce. Everything else of the phase passed its verification (unit 11,428; contract 244; three whole-suite runs of 912; the fingerprint of `public/`).

**What was measured before anything was changed** (`evidence/phase-8/r2/`):

- `probe-1.log`, first three tests. On the production bridge with the window at 5,000: a 9,000-row file with line 5,001 three quarters down the pane has 5,000 rows drawn, the last of them at the pane's bottom edge and not where the layout has it (`dTop` 221 px, alone and in a pane of 31 files), at rest; a 300 px scroll further draws all 9,000 at once; back, the same again. That is F30, read afterwards in `VirtualizedFileDiff.computeRenderRangeFromWindow`. It is not confined to reveals: every file that shows more than 5,000 rows has it at every 5,000th row, since phase 5.
- The same log, the remaining tests: with a row window larger than any file (1e9) the verifier's scenario passes in every position, with a clean audit.
- `probe-lazy-whole-1e9.log`, `probe-whole-where.log`: with that window `lazy-diff.spec.js` under the development bundle reports its 20,100-row file 39.4 px taller than computed, as phase 5 saw. The rows were measured one by one: 15,062 at 17.390625 px, then from row 15,067 on 3,778 at 17.375 and 1,260 at 17.40625, while the column itself is exactly 20,100 times 17.390625 high. F31. Files of 12,000 and 6,000 rows are exact. So "every file whole" is not available, and 5,000 is kept for a measured reason.
- `probe-2-wrapper.log`, `probe-2b-wrapper.log`: the wrapper as a page-side prototype. A 12,000-row file walked across both boundaries, down and up, alone and among 31 files: the pane shows the lines the layout says at every step, audit clean, 0 validator reports; the verifier's navigation resolves in 1.3 s in every position.

**What changed.**

- `public/js/modules/pierre-bridge.js`: `PierreBridge.windowPastDrawnRows` and `PierreBridge.coverPaneWithRowWindow` (F30, H162), installed by the constructor, with one warning when the vendor's class or method is not there; `whenAnnotationRendered` sends the target once more after a pending diff is displayed (H155); `_awaitsDisplay` (the question "mounted, and drawing an older diff", which `_displayWillDraw` asked inline); the comment at `ROWS_PER_ITEM_WINDOW` says why the number is neither smaller nor larger.
- `scripts/pierre-diffs-entry.mjs`: exports `VirtualizedFileDiff` (correction 25); both bundles rebuilt.
- Callers of the shared functions touched (the repository's rule). `_displayWillDraw`: `scrollToAnnotation`, `whenAnnotationRendered`; its answers are unchanged (the same condition, through `_awaitsDisplay`). `whenAnnotationRendered`: `PRManager._revealSteps` only; the added scroll is sent to the same line with the same alignment as the one `_revealSteps` sent a moment before, only when the diff that awaited display is on screen and the card is still not drawn, after the caller's `isCancelled` was asked in that frame. A newer navigation cancels the older one's wait, so two waits do not send against each other. `computeRenderRangeFromWindow`: the vendor's `render`, `getAdvancedStickySpecs` and `applyDocumentChange` (the last one only outside a `CodeView`); all three get the same answer for the same window.

**Tests added, and what each fails on** (`r2/mutations.txt` has the runs):

| Test | Fails when |
| --- | --- |
| Vendor, `row-window.spec.js`: "F30 (vendor defect): with a row window larger than the pane, the rows after a block boundary are not drawn ..." and "... the last rows of an item that ends a few rows after a block boundary, at the end of the pane, are never drawn" | the vendor covers the pane itself (the wrapper can go) |
| Vendor, `row-window.spec.js`: "F31 (vendor defect): rows more than 2^18 px from the viewport are measured a 64th of a pixel off ..." | the vendor's measurement no longer depends on the distance (the row window can be reconsidered) |
| Bridge, `view.spec.js`: "a file of more than 5,000 rows is drawn where the pane is at every position across its block boundaries ..." (twelve positions, both boundaries, both directions) and "the last rows of a file that ends the pane a few rows after a block boundary are drawn" (alone; last of 31) | `windowPastDrawnRows` answers nothing: all three fail |
| Bridge, `annotations.spec.js`: "navigating to a card on a line that was just revealed in a long file", eight cases (H155) | without the wrapper five fail; without the pending diff's row count seven; without the second target the two that need it |
| E2E, `offscreen-mutations.spec.js`: "The last rows of a file a few rows longer than one block of the viewer's rows, at the end of the pane", PR and Local (a 5,006-line added file as the last file; its last line, its 5,000th and a comment on line 5,004 are in the viewport at the pane's end, and the comment is reached from the review panel from the top) | without the wrapper, in both modes (H163 for the test's first form) |
| Unit, `pierre-bridge.test.js`: eight tests of the arithmetic, the installation (once per class, across reloads of the script), the warning, and the second target (sent once, after the display, never after a cancel, never for a file whose diff was on screen all along) | the wiring changes; the behaviour itself is the contract tests' |

| Look check against the saved reference (`visual.log`, the whole matrix) | exit 0. 480 screenshot pairs, none with a pixel a reader could tell apart: 400 equal, 72 context-file shots not compared (D2), 8 accepted; numbers: 1,128 differences, none unaccepted. The same counts as before the fix round |
| Performance against the saved reference (`perf.log`; medians of five runs, PR / Local) | first row 315 → 188 ms / 313 → 177 ms; file elements after scrolling the 60-file corpus 60 → 1; no long task; frame time while scrolling, median 9.9 → 10.2 ms / 10.1 → 10.2 ms, 95th percentile 16.8 → 19.9 ms / 16.8 → 18.4 ms (the machine's load was 1.7 to 3.6 during this run against 1.6 for the reference); a reveal on screen after 11.7 → 40.2 ms / 9.6 → 40.1 ms (F5). None of the corpora of this script has a file of more than 5,000 rows, so the second question to the viewer is never asked in it |
| The verifier's probe specs on the fixed tree (`r2/verifier-probes-on-fixed-tree.log`) | I1, I3, I4: the card is handed over after 1.3 s, drawn, inside the pane after the browser's scroll, 5,006 rows, audit clean, 0 validator reports. I2 (the pending diff's rows not asked) still fails, as it should. Its variants A to H: every row drawn at every step, audit clean, 0 reports |
| The new E2E test repeated (`r2/repeat25-e2e-tail-3.log`, `r2/repeat20-e2e-tail-dev-2.log`) | 50 of 50 on the shipped bundle, 40 of 40 on the development bundle |

The `--reference-checkout` path of `visual.mjs` was not run again: the second checkout was removed at the end of the phase's third session, and `tests/parity/` has not changed since the run that proved it (`final/visual-checkout-smoke.log`).

**Deviations of the fix round.** (1) The bridge wraps a method of a vendor class (H162); the plan and decision D6 had the bridge read vendor internals, not replace one. (2) The entry exports a fifth name (correction 25). (3) Orchestrator note (d) offered "handle it, with a test, or record why it cannot occur": it can occur and is handled, and the handling needed the row window itself corrected, which is wider than the note. (4) F30 and F31 are two more vendor defects for the upstream report and for #638; neither is filed by this phase.

**Left for phase 9.** The evidence package and the comparison with main the user asked for; H119's separator half; the upstream report's two new entries (F30, F31, with F26) and their place in #638; a look at a file of more than 5,000 rows on screen, which no parity corpus has (the E2E and contract tests of F30 are what covers it); the "future 1.7 upgrade" section; the #638 candidates (H143, H147(c), the misdirected expansion of H146); the find-in-page issue. The saved reference set and the earlier phases' evidence are in a scratch directory the operating system cleans (H159).

## 12. The D2 decision: context files are `CodeView` items

Record of the decision of 2026-11-14 and of what was measured for it. The session documents are `D2-AMENDMENT.md` and the amended `PLAN.md` (section 3.15 there is the full design); this section repeats what a later reader needs. Hazards the decision adds are H79 to H92 (section 8); the vendor facts are F20 to F25 (section 4); the gate is G13 (section 5).

**The question.** Where context files render: files and line ranges outside the diff that a chat link, a tour stop, a comment on a non-diff file or the user adds to the diff pane. The plan's first default kept main's table renderer and put them in a non-virtualized footer region after all changed files.

**The decision.** Option B: context files are real `CodeView` items, interleaved by path among the changed files exactly as on main. One renderer. Accepted by the user: the look of context files changes to the vendor's highlighting and separators. Not accepted: any loss of function.

**The design in brief.**

1. One item list, two kinds of record. A context file is a `type: 'diff'` item whose bridge record has `kind: 'context'`; its id is the file path. `PRManager.fileKind(path)` answers the kind from data; `_fileStateKey(path)` builds the persisted `context:<path>` state key.
2. Order is main's: changed files in diff order, each context path (in code-unit order) before the first entry whose path sorts after it.
3. Metadata is `{ ...parseDiffFromFile(file, file), type: 'new' }` with the stored ranges merged in by `PierreContext.mergeContextRanges`. Several rows of one file are one item with one separator per hidden gap.
4. Because a context item is a diff item, everything keyed on `pierreBridge.files.has(path)` takes the path it already has for changed files: comment forms and cards, suggestions, tour stops, external threads, chat about lines, the gutter, line selection, `ensureLinesVisible`, `revealInDiff`, the minimizer. The row-based twins of all of these lose their last user.
5. Expansion is the vendor's: 20 lines per click, Shift+click for a whole gap, and the vendor's own "Expand all" control, shown on context hosts by CSS.
6. Adding or removing a context file at run time is a list replace by the second list writer, `reconcile(entries)`, which keeps every unchanged record, folds manual expansions into ranges first, and restores the first visible line, the line selection, the focused element with its caret, and the hover.
7. `loadContextFiles` becomes data-only with a generation guard (phase 5). `renderDiff` no longer resets the rows.
8. One CSS block, `CONTEXT_CSS` (ten rules, all under `:host(.context-file)`, in `public/js/modules/pierre-styles.js`), and one correction to `pierre-context.js` (section 6, item 10).

**The look, specified.**

| Element | Main | With D2 = B |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Header | Chevron, path, orange `CONTEXT` badge, Viewed, file-comment, chat, dismiss; 45 px | The same markup and height; sticky like a changed file's |
| Left border | Blue, on the wrapper | Blue, on the host |
| Line numbers | Two columns, same number in both | One column |
| Code | highlight.js, line by line | The vendor's Shiki themes, the colours changed files have; the background of an unchanged line of a changed file; no change bars |
| Hidden lines | A row "N hidden lines" with icon buttons; clicking the label reveals the whole gap; between two ranges two such rows and a thin separator | A 32 px grey bar: chevron button(s) at the left, "N unmodified lines", and for a gap of more than 20 lines "Expand all" at the right end; one bar between two ranges |
| Hidden gap of 5 lines or fewer | A gap row | The lines are shown (the rule changed files have, `collapsedContextThreshold: 5`) |
| Comment and chat buttons on a line | Inside the row | The gutter buttons of a changed file, on hover |
| Cards, forms, file-comment zone | The app's own | The same elements, under their line, full width; the zone between header and code |
| Split layout | Unaffected by the toggle | Unaffected: one full-width column, identical to unified |
| Very large (over 400 KB or 12,000 lines) or binary file | Coloured line by line | Not coloured; everything else works |
| While the worker tokenizes | n/a | Rows are plain first, then coloured |
| File ending in a newline | An extra empty last line | No extra line; the count after the last range is one smaller |
| Empty file | One empty row numbered 1 | Header-only item |

**Every function on context lines, and where it comes from after the change.**

| Function | After the change |
| --- | --- |
| Line comment (single and range), edit, delete, restore | Gutter utility and vendor line selection → form annotation → comment annotation; saved with `side: 'RIGHT'` |
| Comment stored with `side: 'LEFT'` on a file that left the diff | Drawn under its line: the bridge publishes every annotation of a context record on the additions side and keeps the stored side in the data |
| Comment or suggestion on a line outside the shown ranges | `ensureLinesVisible` → `addContextRanges` (replaces `expandForSuggestion`) |
| File-level comment, header comment indicator | The zone is the `lineNumber: 0` annotation every item has |
| AI suggestion or finding on a context line; adopt, edit-and-adopt, dismiss, restore; panel click; navigator keys | Suggestion annotation; `revealInDiff` |
| Chat about a line or a selection; header chat button | Gutter chat button; header button |
| Chat link to a context file, a line, a new range, a file not yet shown | `fileKind` and `isLineVisible`, then `ensureContextFile`, then `revealInDiff` |
| Tour stop outside the diff; cleanup when the tour exits | `ensureContextFile`, then the annotation mount path; exit calls `removeContextFile` as today, each ending in a `reconcile` that drops the item and its annotations |
| External thread on such a file (PR mode) | The annotation path with the visibility oracle, file-level fallback |
| Collapse, Viewed, their persistence | `setFileCollapsed` and the viewed toggle under the `context:<path>` key; no wrapper needed |
| Dismiss | The rows of the path from `contextFiles` (not from DOM chunks), deleted as today |
| Sidebar entry, badge, click | Unchanged; the click is a `revealInDiff` call |
| Expansion by step and of a whole gap; start and end of file | Vendor controls, "Expand all" by CSS |
| Reload | Rows and viewed state from the server as today; expansion is view state and is lost, as on main |
| Hunk summaries | None, as on main |

Edge cases, each matched to main unless marked: a file that cannot be fetched has a sidebar entry and nothing in the pane; a binary file is drawn as the text `git show` printed (uncoloured); a path that is also a changed file is never a context item, and its stored rows return when it leaves the diff.

**What is deleted as a result (phase 8; established by reference counts, proven by phase 8's grep gates).** In `pr.js`: `renderPatch`, `parseBlockLines`, `_renderLegacyFileDiffBody`, `renderDiffLine`, `getLineNumber`, `validatePendingEofGaps`, `expandGapContext`, `expandGapRange`, `expandForSuggestion`, the legacy branches of `ensureLinesVisible`, `_loadUserComments` and `showCommentForm`, the row selection delegates; `renderContextFile`, `_buildContextChunkTbody`, `_insertChunkSorted`, `_createChunkSeparator`, `findContextFileWrapper`, the DOM diffing in `loadContextFiles`. Modules: `line-tracker.js` and `gap-coordinates.js` entirely; the gap-row half of `hunk-parser.js`; `DiffRenderer.renderDiffLine` and its highlight.js code; the row-based forms and cards of `comment-manager.js`; the row branches of `suggestion-manager.js`, `external-comment-manager.js`, `tour-renderer.js`, `ChatPanel.js`, `AIPanel.js`. HTML: the highlight.js stylesheets, library and language packs from cdnjs. CSS: the `pr.css` rules for table rows. Kept: the host and header classes (`.d2h-file-wrapper`, `.d2h-file-header`, `.context-file`, `.context-badge`, ...), the parse helpers of `hunk-parser.js` while their callers remain, `FileListMerger`, `DiffContext`, the context-file routes and repository on the server.

**Acceptance checks the decision adds** (no existing bar is lowered): phase 4, the F23 unit and contract tests with the default-engine E2E run and golden dump unchanged, `context-items.spec.js`, gate G13, the context and `reconcile` bridge tests; phase 5, N5's read-only scenarios under `codeview` in both modes, the context stage of the golden dump, a context file in split layout one column as wide as the pane; phase 6, all of N5 under `codeview`; phase 7, N8's context scenarios; phase 8, the grep gates for the table renderer and no highlight.js from a CDN; phase 9, the context golden comparison classified and context files in the live drive through the real routes.

**Planning evidence** (measured before the decision on the real 1.6.4 development bundle, height validator on, with `pr.css`, main's header markup and main's card renderer; session scripts `d2/probe-<n>*.cjs`, results and screenshots in the session's `evidence/d2/`; each is now a contract test, section 4):

| Probe | What it measured | Fact |
| --- | --- | --- |
| D2-1, D2-2 | The zero-change base, a ranged item in unified layout, hover, gutter, selection, scroll targets, annotations, collapse | F20 |
| D2-2b, D2-2c | Each expansion control, Shift+click, the hidden "Expand all", the shape of the expansion map | F22 |
| D2-2d, D2-2e, D2-2f | The layout corruption after two manual expansions, bisected to the line starts; the same sequence on vendor-hydrated metadata (clean); with the starts recomputed (clean) | F23 |
| D2-3, D2-4 | `type: 'new'` in split layout; the final design with `CONTEXT_CSS`: validator 0 and an exact audit in unified and split, light and dark, with wrapped lines, after real clicks and a walk | F21 |
| D2-5 | List replace with a context item inserted above the reader (31 items, pool on): 12.8 ms, 0 px, focus, caret and selection kept, 0 orphans; `parseDiffFromFile` cost and line counting | F24, F25 |
| D2-6 | A hidden gap of 5 lines or fewer is drawn in full at the start, at the end and between ranges; 6 lines keep the separator | the small-gap rule |

Not verified by the planning probes, and where each is closed: `CONTEXT_CSS` with the app's real gutter buttons and real split card layout function (G13, phase 4); whether a list replace loses focus when nothing restores it, and `reconcile` during a pending swap or before the pool is ready (bridge tests, phase 4); a click on the separator label (contract test, phase 4); list-replace cost above 31 items (phase 5 performance run); what main draws for binary, very large and unreadable context files, and the real context routes end to end (phase 5 characterization, phase 9 live drive); the two-column fallback in split layout beyond its picture.
